Real-read implementation design
O-BX may enable the real session read only inside the diagnostic endpoint.
O-BX should replace the static-only endpoint with a guarded implementation:
1. require x-controlled-intake-first-real-session-read-test:true before any session helper call;
2. keep GET only;
3. keep dynamic=force-dynamic;
4. define FIRST_REAL_AUTH_SESSION_RUNTIME_READ_APPROVED=true as const inside the endpoint implementation;
5. call the approved project Auth0/server session helper only after header and approval checks;
6. extract only sanitized identity fields;
7. pass only sanitized data into buildControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeBridge;
8. return sanitized success/failure JSON;
9. keep DB/SQL/AI/write impossible.

Preferred project Auth0 helper
O-BX should use the existing local project Auth0/server helper discovered by evidence.
If the project exposes auth0 from lib/auth0, the preferred call shape should be:
- import { auth0 } from "@/lib/auth0";
- const session = await auth0.getSession();

If the local evidence shows a different wrapper helper, O-BX must use the project wrapper rather than inventing a new Auth0 client.
O-BX must not create a second Auth0Client instance unless the existing project helper is absent and a separate design gate explicitly approves that fallback.

Required O-BX fail-closed order
- missing diagnostic header -> 403 before Auth0/session call;
- wrong diagnostic header -> 403 before Auth0/session call;
- internal approval not true -> 403 before Auth0/session call;
- Auth0/session helper unavailable -> controlled failure;
- helper throws -> controlled failure;
- missing session -> 401 controlled failure;
- missing subject -> controlled failure;
- blank subject -> controlled failure;
- email-only identity -> controlled failure;
- unsupported provider -> controlled failure;
- unsafe extraction/bridge result -> controlled failure.

Required O-BX session extraction
O-BX must extract only:
- authSubject from session.user.sub, session.sub, session.user.authSubject, or session.authSubject;
- provider as auth0;
- email from session.user.email or session.email only if available and safe;
- sessionId from session.sid, session.user.sid, session.sessionId only if available and safe;
- issuedAt/expiresAt from safe numeric/date fields only if available.

O-BX must not put the raw session into bridge or response.
O-BX must not return full session.
O-BX must not return full user/profile object.
O-BX must not return tokens.

Required sanitized bridge input
O-BX should construct a minimal injected project auth session like:
- provider: "auth0"
- sub/authSubject: sanitized subject
- email: sanitized email if present
- sessionId: sanitized session id if present
- user: { sub: sanitized subject, email?: sanitized email }

Then call:
- buildControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeBridge({
    connectionApproved: true,
    runtimeReadApproved: true,
    authBoundary: "project_auth0_server_session",
    requestSource: "controlled-intake-first-real-auth-session-runtime-read-test-real-read-endpoint",
    injectedProjectAuthSession: sanitizedSession
  })

Required O-BX success response
Authenticated browser + valid header + safe session + bridge success:
- HTTP 200
- ok=true
- stage="first_real_auth_session_runtime_read_test"
- layer="controlled-activity-intake-first-real-auth-session-runtime-read-test-v1"
- requestSource
- sessionPresent=true
- provider="auth0"
- authSubject present and non-empty
- email only if present and safe
- identity.provider="auth0"
- identity.authSubject present and non-empty
- connectionBridgeStatus.connectionOk=true
- connectionBridgeStatus.runtimeOk=true
- connectionBridgeStatus.staticIdentityOk=true
- guardrails.debugEndpointOnly=true
- guardrails.diagnosticReadOnly=true
- guardrails.controlledIntakeRouteChanged=false
- guardrails.realAuth0SessionReadAllowed=true
- guardrails.realAuth0SessionReadExecuted=true
- guardrails.auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=true

Required O-BX missing-session response
Unauthenticated browser + valid header:
- HTTP 401
- ok=false
- code=CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED
- sessionPresent=false
- realAuth0SessionReadExecuted=true
- auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=true
- no tokens
- no raw session
- no DB/SQL/AI/write activity

Required O-BX missing-header response
Missing or wrong diagnostic header:
- HTTP 403
- ok=false
- code=CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_DEBUG_HEADER_REQUIRED
- no Auth0/session helper call
- realAuth0SessionReadExecuted=false
- auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=false
- dbReadExecuted=false
- dbWriteExecuted=false
- sqlExecuted=false
- aiCallExecuted=false
- activityEventsInserted=false

Required O-BX helper failure response
Helper throws:
- HTTP 500
- ok=false
- code=CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_READ_FAILED
- sanitized message only
- no raw exception
- no raw session
- no tokens

Required O-BX bridge failure response
Bridge failure:
- HTTP 401/403/500 depending on bridge failure code
- ok=false
- bridge failure code preserved when safe
- sanitized message only
- no raw session
- no tokens

Required next browser verification after O-BX
O-BY should perform browser-authenticated real-read verification.
O-BY should include a console script or route test instructions that:

Important limitation after O-BW
O-BW approves only the real-read implementation step in the diagnostic endpoint.
It does not execute a real Auth0/session runtime read.
It does not approve controlled-intake route integration.
It does not approve Supabase reads.
It does not approve production write mode.
It does not approve activity_events insert.
It does not approve AI or semantic persistence.

Recommended next corridor
- O-BX: diagnostic endpoint real-read implementation, still no DB/SQL/AI/write and no browser execution by the script.
- O-BY: browser-authenticated real-read verification.
- Later separate gates: server-side app user mapping read, ownership/context resolution, controlled-intake route integration, production write proof.

Still forbidden after O-BW
- Do not integrate runtime adapter into controlled-intake route.ts.
- Do not execute real Auth0/session runtime reads from controlled-intake route.ts.
- Do not execute DB reads.
- Do not execute DB writes.
- Do not execute SQL.
- Do not add Supabase insert.
- Do not add production write route.
- Do not trust client-provided ownership fields.
- Do not call AI.
- Do not run Category Derivation.
- Do not persist category candidates.
- Do not create Value Objects.
- Do not write state facts, deltas, or snapshots.
